currency_value_evolution_list = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            #has_modifier = has_financial_center
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = currency_value_evolution_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root  = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = currency_value_evolution_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_global_variable_list = currency_value_evolution_list_ordered_1
            if = {
                ordered_in_list = {
                    variable = list_before_order
                    order_by = money_value_dif_1_final
                    max = 15
                    check_range_bounds = no
                    root = {
                        add_to_global_variable_list = {
                            name = currency_value_evolution_list_ordered_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        clear_variable_list = currency_value_evolution_list
    }
}
independent_economies_list = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			or = {
				zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no
			}
			is_revolutionary = no
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = independent_economies_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root  = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = independent_economies_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_global_variable_list = independent_economies_list_ordered_1
            if = {
                ordered_in_list = {
                    variable = list_before_order
                    order_by = money_value_in_gold
                    max = 100
                    check_range_bounds = no
                    root = {
                        add_to_global_variable_list = {
                            name = independent_economies_list_ordered_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        clear_variable_list = independent_economies_list
    }
}
dependent_economies_list = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			is_revolutionary = no
			NOT = {
				zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
			}

money_supply_variable_list = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
          is_reference_type = 1
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = money_supply_variable_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root  = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = money_supply_variable_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_global_variable_list = money_supply_variable_list_ordered_1
            if = {
                ordered_in_list = {
                    variable = list_before_order
                    order_by = total_money_supply
                    max = 10
                    check_range_bounds = no
                    root = {
                        add_to_global_variable_list = {
                            name = money_supply_variable_list_ordered_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        clear_variable_list = money_supply_variable_list
    }
}
national_capacity_variable_list = {
	clear_variable_list = national_capacity_variable_list
	clear_variable_list = reference_currency_list_1
	# the yearly pick's great powers, for the log -- E&F's rating note (candidate: 6+), cover (candidate: 0.25+),
	# reserves in gold (the order), standard (1 gold, 2 silver, 3 bimetallism, 4 gold exchange), the reference now. Line
	# EFE.
	every_country = {
		limit = { country_rank >= rank_value:great_power }
		if = { limit = { zz_ef_logs_on = yes } debug_log = "EFE|[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]|[THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]|note [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.Var('country_credit_note_fixe').GetValue|0]|cover [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('zz_ef_cb_cover')|3]|cap_g [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('national_capacity_in_gold')|0]|std [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('zz_ef_std_code')|0]|cb [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('zz_ef_has_cb_v')|0]|ref [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('zz_ef_is_reference_v')|0]" }
	}
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            has_modifier = has_central_bank
			not = {
				or = {
					market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
					is_subject_custom_trigger = yes
				}
			}
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = national_capacity_variable_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root  = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = national_capacity_variable_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_global_variable_list = national_capacity_variable_list_ordered_1
            ordered_in_list = {
                variable = list_before_order
                order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
                min = 0
                max = 10
                check_range_bounds = no
                root = {
                    add_to_global_variable_list = {
                        name = national_capacity_variable_list_ordered_1
                        target = prev
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { prev = { zz_ef_reference_candidate = yes } }
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = reference_currency_list_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_variable_list = reference_currency_country_1
            # the log of the candidates and the pick (EFE)
            every_in_list = {
                variable = reference_currency_list_1
                if = { limit = { zz_ef_logs_on = yes } debug_log = "EFE|[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]|[THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]|cand|cap_g [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('national_capacity_in_gold')|0]" }
            }
            ordered_in_list = {
                variable = reference_currency_list_1
                order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
                # the first -- positions count from 0, position alone (vanilla's form for the top)
                position = 0
                check_range_bounds = no
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = reference_currency_country_1
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            } 

			every_in_list = {
				variable = reference_currency_country_1
				if = { limit = { zz_ef_logs_on = yes } debug_log = "EFE|[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]|[THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]|pick" }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_in_list = { variable = reference_currency_country_1 } }
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = global_monetary_reference
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country
			}
            every_in_list = {
                variable = national_capacity_variable_list 
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = global_monetary_reference } remove_modifier = global_monetary_reference }
            } 

            every_in_list = {
                variable = reference_currency_country_1 
				add_modifier = {
					name = global_monetary_reference
				}

				if = {
					limit = { 
						not = { 
							scope:target_country = this 
						}
					} 
					#Event for player
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
					save_scope_as = target_country

					every_country = {
						limit = {
							is_player = yes 
						}
						trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.33
					}   
				} 
			}
            }
			# no candidate (Д.R8б.27): the reference stays with its country, fiat too, while it exists; with no
			# reference at all -- the biggest great power with a CB by its reserves in gold, any standard
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_country = { has_modifier = global_monetary_reference } } }
				ordered_country = {
					limit = {
						country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
						has_modifier = has_central_bank
					}
					order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
					max = 1
					check_range_bounds = no
					add_modifier = { name = global_monetary_reference }
					if = { limit = { zz_ef_logs_on = yes } debug_log = "EFE|[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]|[THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]|pick|no_candidate_no_reference|std [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('zz_ef_std_code')|0]" }
				}
			}
			else = {
				every_country = {
					limit = { has_modifier = global_monetary_reference }
					if = { limit = { zz_ef_logs_on = yes } debug_log = "EFE|[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]|[THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]|keep|no_candidate|std [THIS.GetCountry.MakeScope.ScriptValue('zz_ef_std_code')|0]" }
				}
			}
        }
    }
}
is_subject_list = {  #local
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            or = {
                is_subject_of = root
                #
                #is_subject_type = subject_type_protectorate
            }
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = is_subject_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root  = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = is_subject_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_variable_list = is_subject_list_ordered_1
            if = {
                ordered_in_list = {
                    variable = list_before_order
                    order_by = prestige
                    max = 10
                    check_range_bounds = no
                    root = {
                        add_to_variable_list = {
                            name = is_subject_list_ordered_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        clear_variable_list = is_subject_list
    }
}

exports_imports_variable_list = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            has_modifier = has_central_bank
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no
			import_value_in_currency > 0
			global_country_ranking < 20
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = exports_imports_variable_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root  = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = exports_imports_variable_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_global_variable_list = exports_imports_variable_list_ordered_1
            if = {
                ordered_in_list = {
                    variable = list_before_order
                    order_by = trade_balance_convert_gold #trade_balance_in_gold
                    max = 20
                    check_range_bounds = no
                    root = {
                        add_to_global_variable_list = {
                            name = exports_imports_variable_list_ordered_1
                            target = prev
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        clear_variable_list = exports_imports_variable_list
    }
}

central_bank_debt_variable_list = {
	#changement de princuipe ce n'azi plus la dette de la bank qui compter
	#modifier l'odre de pays acheter en premier à l'avenir avec un score ?

    every_country = {
        limit = {
			gold_loaned_to_central_bank < total_debt_80 #tout les pays avec une dette non comblé à au minimum 80% sont éligible
			central_bank_metal_reserves_state_to_ensure_the_conversion_pourcent < 0.75 #donc besoin investissement

			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			zz_ef_bl_room > 0
			not = {
				has_modifier = country_credit_rating_D
			} 
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = central_bank_debt_variable_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = central_bank_debt_variable_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_global_variable_list = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
			ordered_in_list = {
				variable = list_before_order
				order_by = remaining_debt
				max = 100
				check_range_bounds = no
				root = {
					add_to_global_variable_list = {
						name = central_bank_debt_variable_list_ordered_1
						target = prev
					}
				}
			}
        }
        clear_variable_list = central_bank_debt_variable_list
    }
}
country_index_variable_list = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            has_modifier = has_financial_center
        }
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = country_index_variable_list
                target = prev
            }
        }
    }
    root  = {
        if = {
            clear_variable_list = list_before_order
            every_in_list = {
                variable = country_index_variable_list
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = list_before_order
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_global_variable_list = country_index_variable_list_ordered_1
            ordered_in_list = {
                variable = list_before_order
                order_by = country_indice_in_gold #country_indice
                max = 10
                check_range_bounds = no
                root = {
                    add_to_global_variable_list = {
                        name = country_index_variable_list_ordered_1
                        target = prev
                    }
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = financial_power_list_1
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
            clear_variable_list = financial_power_country_1
            ordered_in_list = {
                variable = financial_power_list_1
                order_by = country_indice_in_gold #country_indice
                min = 0
                max = 1
                check_range_bounds = no
                root = {
                    add_to_variable_list = {
                        name = financial_power_country_1
                        target = prev
                    }
                }
            }
        
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = global_financial_power
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country
			}
            every_in_list = {
                variable = country_index_variable_list 
                if = { limit = { has_modifier = global_financial_power } remove_modifier = global_financial_power }
            } 

            every_in_list = {
                variable = financial_power_country_1 
				add_modifier = {
					name = global_financial_power
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						not = { 
							scope:target_country = this 
						}
					} 
					#Event for player
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
					save_scope_as = target_country

					every_country = {
						limit = {
							is_player = yes 
						}
						trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.34
					}   
				}   
            }
        }
        clear_variable_list = country_index_variable_list
    }
}

global_arbitrage_bank_variable_list = { 
	every_country = {
		every_company = {
			limit = {
				or = {
					type = company_type:company_Rothschild_Bank_gbr  
					type = company_type:company_Rothschild_Bank_fra  
					type = company_type:company_Rothschild_Bank_ger  
					type = company_type:company_Rothschild_Bank_aus  
					type = company_type:company_Rothschild_Bank_ita  
					type = company_type:company_BanqueDeParisEtDesPaysBas  
					type = company_type:company_BankJPMorgan  
					type = company_type:company_DeutscheBank  
					type = company_type:company_Bankenverein  
					type = company_type:company_BancaCommercialeItaliana  
					type = company_type:company_DeNederlandscheBank  
					type = company_type:company_amsterdamschebank  
					type = company_type:company_RotterdamscheBankvereeniging  
					type = company_type:company_Mitsubishiexchangehousebank   
				}
			}
			root = {
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = global_arbitrage_bank_variable_list
					target = prev
				}
			}
		} 
	}  
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = global_arbitrage_bank_variable_list
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = gdp_var
						max = 100
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
						}
					}
				}
			}
			clear_variable_list = global_arbitrage_bank_variable_list
		}
	}
}

#begin_tag_2
#dinar
dinar_currency_law_list = {
	clear_variable_list = dinar_currency_law_list
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:dinar
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = dinar_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = dinar_leading_currency_type }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = root }
			root = {
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = dinar_currency_law_list
					target = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = dinar_currency_law_list
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_global_variable_list = dinar_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = dinar_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = dinar_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = dinar_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = dinar_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = dinar_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}
			#clear_variable_list = dinar_currency_list
		}
	}
	if = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:dinar
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = dinar_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = dinar_leading_currency_type }
		}
	}
}

		limit = {
			var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:eco_ariary
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = eco_ariary_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = eco_ariary_leading_currency_type }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = root }
			root = {
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = eco_ariary_currency_law_list
					target = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = eco_ariary_currency_law_list
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_global_variable_list = eco_ariary_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = eco_ariary_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = eco_ariary_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = eco_ariary_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = eco_ariary_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = eco_ariary_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}
			#clear_variable_list = eco_ariary_currency_list
		}
	}
	if = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:eco_ariary
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = eco_ariary_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = eco_ariary_leading_currency_type }
		}
	}
}

					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_global_variable_list = franc_belgian_franc_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = franc_belgian_franc_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = franc_belgian_franc_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = franc_belgian_franc_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = franc_belgian_franc_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = franc_belgian_franc_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}
			#clear_variable_list = franc_belgian_franc_currency_list
		}
	}
	if = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:franc_belgian_franc
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			}

#krone_czech_koruna
krone_czech_koruna_currency_law_list = {
	clear_variable_list = krone_czech_koruna_currency_law_list
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:krone_czech_koruna
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = krone_czech_koruna_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = krone_czech_koruna_leading_currency_type }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = root }
			root = {
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = krone_czech_koruna_currency_law_list
					target = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = krone_czech_koruna_currency_law_list
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_global_variable_list = krone_czech_koruna_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = krone_czech_koruna_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = krone_czech_koruna_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = krone_czech_koruna_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = krone_czech_koruna_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = krone_czech_koruna_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}

		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = lira_currency_law_list
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_global_variable_list = lira_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = lira_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = lira_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = lira_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = lira_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = lira_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}
			#clear_variable_list = lira_currency_list
		}
	}
	if = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:lira
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = lira_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = lira_leading_currency_type }
		}
	}
}

#peso_bolivien_peso
peso_bolivien_peso_currency_law_list = {
	clear_variable_list = peso_bolivien_peso_currency_law_list
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:peso_bolivien_peso
			has_central_bank_SS_BS_GS_GES_NISO = yes
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = peso_bolivien_peso_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = peso_bolivien_peso_leading_currency_type }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = root }
			root = {
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = peso_bolivien_peso_currency_law_list
					target = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = peso_bolivien_peso_currency_law_list
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_global_variable_list = peso_bolivien_peso_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = peso_bolivien_peso_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = peso_bolivien_peso_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = peso_bolivien_peso_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = peso_bolivien_peso_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = peso_bolivien_peso_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}
			#clear_variable_list = peso_bolivien_peso_currency_list
		}
	}
	if = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:peso_bolivien_peso
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = peso_bolivien_peso_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = peso_bolivien_peso_leading_currency_type }
		}
	}
}

				clear_global_variable_list = pound_sterling_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = pound_sterling_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = pound_sterling_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = pound_sterling_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = pound_sterling_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = pound_sterling_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}
			#clear_variable_list = pound_sterling_currency_list
		}
	}
	if = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:pound_sterling
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = pound_sterling_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = pound_sterling_leading_currency_type }
		}
	}
}

				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = spe_lithuanian_litas_currency_law_list
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_global_variable_list = spe_lithuanian_litas_currency_law_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = national_capacity_in_gold
						max = 1
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_global_variable_list = {
								name = spe_lithuanian_litas_currency_law_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_in_global_list = {
					variable = spe_lithuanian_litas_currency_law_list_ordered_1
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spe_lithuanian_litas_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = spe_lithuanian_litas_leading_currency_type }
					add_modifier = {
						name = spe_lithuanian_litas_leading_currency_type
					}
				}
			}
			#clear_variable_list = spe_lithuanian_litas_currency_list
		}
	}
	if = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:spe_lithuanian_litas
				market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = spe_lithuanian_litas_leading_currency_type } remove_modifier = spe_lithuanian_litas_leading_currency_type }
		}
	}
}

#begin_tag_1
#dinar
dinar_c_global_stokpile_variable_list = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
            has_modifier = has_central_bank
			stockpiling_dinar_state > 0
		}
		root = {
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name = dinar_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_1
				target = prev
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = dinar_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_1
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_variable_list = dinar_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = dinar_c_total_stokpile
						max = 10
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_variable_list = {
								name = dinar_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			clear_variable_list = money_supply_variable_list
		}
	}
}

				clear_variable_list = franc_french_franc_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1

						max = 10
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_variable_list = {
								name = franc_french_franc_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_variable_list = leon_leu_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
				if = {

						order_by = leon_leu_c_total_stokpile
						max = 10
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {

							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_variable_list = peso_cuban_peso_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = peso_cuban_peso_c_total_stokpile
						max = 10
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_variable_list = {
								name = peso_cuban_peso_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			clear_variable_list = money_supply_variable_list
		}
	}
}

						order_by = thaler_prussian_thaler_c_total_stokpile
						max = 10
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_variable_list = {
								name = thaler_prussian_thaler_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			clear_variable_list = money_supply_variable_list
		}
	}
}
#thaler_saxon_thaler
thaler_saxon_thaler_c_global_stokpile_variable_list = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
            has_modifier = has_central_bank
			stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state > 0
		}
		root = {
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name = thaler_saxon_thaler_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_1
				target = prev
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = root }
		root  = {
			if = {
				clear_variable_list = list_before_order
				every_in_list = {
					variable = thaler_saxon_thaler_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_1
					root = {
						add_to_variable_list = {
							name = list_before_order
							target = prev
						}
					}
				}
				clear_variable_list = thaler_saxon_thaler_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
				if = {
					ordered_in_list = {
						variable = list_before_order
						order_by = thaler_saxon_thaler_c_total_stokpile
						max = 10
						check_range_bounds = no
						root = {
							add_to_variable_list = {
								name = thaler_saxon_thaler_c_global_stokpile_variable_list_ordered_1
								target = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			clear_variable_list = money_supply_variable_list
		}
	}
}
